const int n) {\n    __shared__ double sx[TILE];\n    __shared__ double sy[TILE];\n    __shared__ double sz[TILE];\n\n    const int tid = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    const int base = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * (TILE * BPT) + tid;\n\n    double xi[BPT], yi[BPT], zi[BPT];\n    double fx[BPT], fy[BPT], fz[BPT];\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int k = 0; k < BPT; ++k) {\n        const int i = base + k * TILE;\n        const bool active = i < n;\n        xi[k] = active ? px[i] : 0.0;\n        yi[k] = active ? py[i] : 0.0;\n        zi[k] = active ? pz[i] : 0.0;\n        fx[k] = 0.0;\n        fy[k] = 0.0;\n        fz[k] = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    for (int tileStart = 0; tileStart < n; tileStart += TILE) {\n        const int count = min(TILE, n - tileStart);\n        const int j = tileStart + tid;\n\n        __syncthreads();\n        if (tid < count) {\n            sx[tid] = px[j];\n            sy[tid] = py[j];\n            sz[tid] = pz[j];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n#pragma unroll 4\n        for (int jj = 0; jj < count; ++jj) {\n            const double xj = sx[jj];\n            const double yj = sy[jj];\n            const double zj = sz[jj];\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int k = 0; k < BPT; ++k) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[k];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[k];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[k];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                fx[k] += dx * invDist3;\n                fy[k] += dy * invDist3;\n                fz[k] += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n      }\n    }\n}\n\n__global__ void integrateBodiesKernel(double* __restrict__ px, double* __restrict__ py, double* __restrict__ pz,\n                                      const double* __restrict__ vx, const double* __restrict__ vy,\n                                      const double* __restrict__ vz, const int n) {\n    const int i = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * static_cast<int>(blockDim.x) + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    if (i < n) {\n        px[i] += vx[i] * DT;\n        py[i] += vy[i] * DT;\n        pz[i] += vz[i] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\n// Potential energy contribution of body i against all j > i, again summed in ascending j order.\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(ENERGY_BLOCK) void potentialEnergyKernel(const double* __restrict__ px,\n                                                                      const double* __restrict__ py,\n                                                                      const double* __restrict__ pz,\n                                                                      double* __restrict__ partial, const int n) {\n    const int i = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * ENERGY_BLOCK + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    if (i >= n) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const double xi = px[i];\n    const double yi = py[i];\n    const double zi = pz[i];\n\n    double sum = 0.0;\n    for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n        const double dx = px[j] - xi;\n        const double dy = py[j] - yi;\n        const double dz = pz[j] - zi;\n        const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n        sum += 1.0 / dist;\n    }\n    partial[i] = sum;\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const DeviceBodies& dev) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    const int n = static_cast<int>(bodies.size());\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    if (n == 0) {\n        return energy;\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies), row sums computed on the GPU\n    double* dPartial = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dPartial, static_cast<size_t>(n) * sizeof(double)));\n    potentialEnergyKernel<<<(n + ENERGY_BLOCK - 1) / ENERGY_BLOCK, ENERGY_BLOCK>>>(dev.px, dev.py, dev.pz, dPartial, n);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n    std::vector<double> partial(n);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(partial.data(), dPartial, static_cast<size_t>(n) * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dPartial));\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        energy -= partial[i];\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if       printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid with blocks.\nstatic void launchComputeForces(const DeviceBodies& dev, const int n, const int numSMs) {\n    const int desiredBlocks = 2 * numSMs;\n    const auto blocksFor = [&](const int bpt) { const int n, const int chunkTiles) {\n    __shared__ double sx[TILE];\n    __shared__ double sy[TILE];\n    __shared__ double sz[TILE];\n\n    const int tid = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    const chunkTiles * TILE) : n;\n\n    double xi[BPT], yi[BPT], zi[BPT];\n    double fx[BPT], fy[BPT], fz[BPT];\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int k = 0; k < BPT; ++k) {\n        const int i = base + k * TILE;\n        const bool active = i < n;\n        xi[k] = active ? px[i] : 0.0;\n        yi[k] = active ? py[i] : 0.0;\n        zi[k] = active ? pz[i] : 0.0;\n        fx[k] = 0.0;\n        fy[k] = 0.0;\n        fz[k] = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    for (int tileStart = jBegin; tileStart < jEnd; tileStart += TILE) {\n        const int count = min(TILE, jEnd - tileStart);\n        const int j = tileStart + tid;\n\n        __syncthreads();\n        if (tid < count) {\n            sx[tid] = px[j];\n            sy[tid] = py[j];\n            sz[tid] = pz[j];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n#pragma unroll 4\n        for (int jj = 0; jj < count; ++jj) {\n            const double xj = sx[jj];\n            const double yj = sy[jj];\n            const double zj = sz[jj];\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int k = 0; k < BPT; ++k) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[k];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[k];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[k];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                fx[k] += dx * invDist3;\n                fy[k] += dy * invDist3;\n                fz[k] += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int k = 0; k < BPT; ++k) {\n        const int i = base + k * TILE;\n        if (i < n) {\n            vx[i] += DT * fx[k];\n            vy[i] += DT * fy[k];\n            vz[i] += DT * fz[k];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n__global__ void integrateBodiesKernel(double* __restrict__ px, double* __restrict__ py, double* __restrict__ pz,\n                                      const double* __restrict__ vx, const double* __restrict__ vy,\n                                      const double* __restrict__ vz, const int n) {\n    const int i = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * static_cast<int>(blockDim.x) + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    if (i < n) {\n        px[i] += vx[i] * DT;\n        py[i] += vy[i] * DT;\n        pz[i] += vz[i] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\n// Potential energy contribution of body i against all j > i, again summed in ascending j order.\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(ENERGY_BLOCK) void potentialEnergyKernel(const double* __restrict__ px,\n                                                                      const double* __restrict__ py,\n                                                                      const double* __restrict__ pz,\n                                                                      double* __restrict__ partial, const int n) {\n    const int i = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * ENERGY_BLOCK + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    if (i >= n) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const double xi = px[i];\n    const double yi = py[i];\n    const double zi = pz[i];\n\n    double sum = 0.0;\n    for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n        const double dx = px[j] - xi;\n        const double dy = py[j] - yi;\n        const double dz = pz[j] - zi;\n        const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n        sum += 1.0 / dist;\n    }\n    partial[i] = sum;\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const DeviceBodies& dev) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    const int n = static_cast<int>(bodies.size());\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    if (n == 0) {\n        return energy;\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies), row sums computed on the GPU\n    double* dPartial = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dPartial, static_cast<size_t>(n) * sizeof(double)));\n    potentialEnergyKernel<<<(n + ENERGY_BLOCK - 1) / ENERGY_BLOCK, ENERGY_BLOCK>>>(dev.px, dev.py, dev.pz, dPartial, n);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n    std::vector<double> partial(n);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(partial.data(), dPartial, static_cast<size_t>(n) * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dPartial));\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        energy -= partial[i];\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Launch the force kernel with as many bodies per thread as the problem size allows while still\n// filling the GPU with blocks.\nstatic void launchComputeForces(const DeviceBodies& dev, const int n, const int numSMs) {\n    const int desiredBlocks = 2 * numSMs;\n    const auto blocksFor = [&](const int bpt) { computeForcesKernel<1><<<blocksFor(1), TILE>>>(dev.px, dev.py, dev.pz, dev.vx, dev.vy, dev.vz, n);\n    }\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n nullptr;\n};\n\n// One thread computes the total force acting on BPT (bodies-per-thread) bodies. Body positions are\n// streamed through shared memory in tiles so that each position is read from global memory only once\n// per block; holding several bodies per thread additionally amortizes the shared-memory traffic and\n// exposes instruction-level parallelism, which matters for the long-latency FP64 pipeline.\n//\n// The inner loop visits j in ascending order for every i, i.e. forces are accumulated in exactly the\n// same order as in the sequential reference implementation.\n//\n// SPLIT == false: every block covers the whole j range and directly updates the velocities.\n// SPLIT == true:  the j range is additionally partitioned over blockIdx.y (needed to fill the GPU when\n//                 there are fewer bodies than desired threads); each block stores the partial force of\n//                 its j chunk, which reduceForcesKernel then sums up in ascending chunk order.\ntemplate <int BPT, bool SPLIT>\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(TILE) void computeForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ px,\n                                                            const double* __restrict__ py,\n                                                            const double* __restrict__ pz, double* __restrict__ vx,\n                                                            double* __restrict__ vy, double* __restrict__ vz,\n                                                            const int n, const int chunkTiles) {\n    __shared__ double sx[TILE];\n    __shared__ double sy[TILE];\n    __shared__ double sz[TILE];\n\n    const int tid = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    const int base = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * (TILE * BPT) + tid;\n\n    const int jBegin = SPLIT ? static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * chunkTiles * TILE : 0;\n    const int jEnd = SPLIT ? min(n, jBegin + chunkTiles * TILE) : n;\n\n    double xi[BPT], yi[BPT], zi[BPT];\n    double fx[BPT], fy[BPT], fz[BPT];\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int k = 0; k < BPT; ++k) {\n        const int i = base + k * TILE;\n        const bool active = i < n;\n        xi[k] = active ? px[i] : 0.0;\n        yi[k] = active ? py[i] : 0.0;\n        zi[k] = active ? pz[i] : 0.0;\n        fx[k] = 0.0;\n        fy[k] = 0.0;\n     __syncthreads();\n        if (tid < count) {\n            sx[tid] = px[j];\n            sy[tid] = py[j];\n            sz[tid] = pz[j];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n#pragma unroll 4\n        for (int jj = 0; jj < count; ++jj) {\n            const double xj = sx[jj];\n            const double yj = sy[jj];\n            const double zj = sz[jj];\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int k = 0; k < BPT; ++k) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[k];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[k];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[k];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                fx[k] += dx * invDist3;\n                fy[k] += dy * invDist3;\n                fz[k] += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int k = 0; k < BPT; ++k) {\n        const (static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * 3) * static_cast<size_t>(n) + i;\n                vx[off] = fx[k];\n                vx[off + n] = fy[k];\n                vx[off + 2 * n] = fz[k];\n            } else {\n                vx[i] += DT * fx[k];\n                vy[i] += DT * fy[k];\n                vz[i] += DT * fz[k];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Sums the per-chunk partial forces in ascending chunk (i.e. ascending j) order and advances the\n// velocities.\n__global__ void reduceForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ partial, double* __restrict__ vx,\n                                  double* __restrict__ vy, double* __restrict__ vz, const int n,\n                                  const int numChunks) {\n    const int i = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * static_cast<int>(blockDim.x) + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    if (i >= n) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;\n    for (int c = 0; c < numChunks; ++c) {\n Body positions are\n// streamed through shared memory in tiles so that each position is read from global memory only once\n// per block; holding several bodies per thread additionally amortizes the shared-memory traffic and\n// exposes instruction-level parallelism, which matters for the long-latency FP64 pipeline.\n//\n// The inner loop visits j in ascending order for every i, i.e. forces are Launch configuration for the force computation: use as many bodies per thread as the problem size\n// allows while still filling the GPU with blocks, and fall back to splitting the j loop across blocks\n// once there are not even enough bodies for one thread each.\nstruct ForcePlan {\n    int bodiesPerThread = 1;\n    int iBlocks = 0;\n    int numChunks = 1; // j chunks; > 1 selects the split         // threads per block == shared-memory tile of bodies\nconstexpr int ENERGY_BLOCK = 256; // threads per block for the energy kernel\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    double x, y, z;\n    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                                               \\\n    do {                                                                                                               \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call); Body positions are\n// streamed through shared memory in tiles so that each position is read from global memory only once\n// per block; holding several bodies per thread additionally amortizes the shared-memory traffic and\n// exposes instruction-level parallelism, which matters for the long-latency FP64 pipeline.\n//\n// The inner loop visits j in ascending order for every i, i.e. forces are accumulated in exactly the\n// same order as in the sequential reference implementation.\n//\n// SPLIT == false: every block covers the whole j range and directly updates the velocities.\n// SPLIT == true:  the j range is additionally partitioned over blockIdx.y (needed to fill the GPU when\n//                 there are fewer bodies than desired threads); each block stores the partial force of\n//                 its j chunk, which reduceForcesKernel then sums up in ascending chunk order.\ntemplate <int BPT, bool SPLIT>\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(TILE) void computeForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ px,\n                                                            const double* __restrict__ py,\n                                                            const double* __restrict__ pz, double* __restrict__ vx,\n                                                            double* __restrict__ vy, double* __restrict__ vz,\n                                                            const int n, const int chunkTiles) {\n    __shared__ double sx[TILE];\n    __shared__ double sy[TILE];\n    __shared__ double sz[TILE];\n\n    const int tid = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    const chunkTiles * TILE) : n;\n\n    double xi[BPT], yi[BPT], zi[BPT];\n    double fx[BPT], fy[BPT], fz[BPT];\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int k = 0; k < BPT; ++k) {\n        const int i = base + k * TILE;\n        const bool active = i < n;\n        xi[k] = active ? px[i] : 0.0;\n        yi[k] = active ? py[i] : 0.0;\n        zi[k] = active ? pz[i] : 0.0;\n        fx[k] = 0.0;\n        fy[k] = 0.0;\n        fz[k] = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    for (int tileStart = jBegin; tileStart < jEnd; tileStart += TILE) {\n        const int count = min(TILE, jEnd - tileStart);\n        const int j = tileStart + tid;\n\n        __syncthreads();\n        if (tid < count) {\n            sx[tid] = px[j];\n            sy[tid] = py[j];\n            sz[tid] = pz[j];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n#pragma unroll 4\n        for (int jj = 0; jj < count; ++jj) {\n            const double xj = sx[jj];\n            const double yj = sy[jj];\n            const double zj = sz[jj];\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int k = 0; k < BPT; ++k) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[k];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[k];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[k];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                fx[k] += dx * invDist3;\n                fy[k] += dy * invDist3;\n                fz[k] += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int k = 0; k < BPT; ++k) {\n        const (static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * 3) * static_cast<size_t>(n) + i;\n                vx[off] = fx[k];\n                vx[off + n] = fy[k];\n                vx[off + 2 * n] = fz[k];\n            } else {\n                vx[i] += DT * fx[k];\n                vy[i] += DT * fy[k];\n                vz[i] += DT * fz[k];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Sums the per-chunk partial forces in ascending chunk (i.e. ascending j) order and advances the\n// velocities.\n__global__ void reduceForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ partial, double* __restrict__ vx,\n                                  double* __restrict__ vy, double* __restrict__ vz, const int n,\n                                  const int numChunks) {\n    const int i = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * static_cast<int>(blockDim.x) + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    if (i >= n) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;\n    for (int c = 0; c < numChunks; ++c) {\n             const double* __restrict__ vx, const double* __restrict__ vy,\n                                      const double* __restrict__ vz, const int n) {\n    const int i = double* __restrict__ pz,\n                                                                      double* __restrict__ partial, const int n) {\n    const int i = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * ENERGY_BLOCK + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    if (i >= n) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const double xi = px[i];\n    const double yi = py[i];\n    const double zi = pz[i];\n\n    double sum = 0.0;\n    for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n        const double dx = px[j] - xi;\n        const double dy = py[j] - yi;\n        const double dz = pz[j] - zi;\n        const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n        sum += 1.0 / dist;\n    }\n    partial[i] = sum;\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const DeviceBodies& dev) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    const int n = static_cast<int>(bodies.size());\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    if (n == 0) {\n        return energy;\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies), row sums computed on the GPU\n    double* dPartial = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dPartial, static_cast<size_t>(n) * sizeof(double)));\n energy -= partial[i];\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    for (const !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n            return printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v Launch configuration for the force computation: use as many bodies per thread as the problem size\n// allows while still filling the GPU with blocks, and fall back to splitting the j loop across blocks\n// once there are not even enough bodies for one thread each.\nstruct ForcePlan {\n    int bodiesPerThread = 1;\n    int iBlocks = 0;\n    int numChunks = 1; // j chunks; > 1 selects the split kernel\n    int chunkTiles = 0;\n};\n\nstatic ForcePlan makeForcePlan(const int n, const int numSMs) {\n    const auto blocksFor = [&](const int bpt) { return (n + TILE * bpt - 1) / (TILE * bpt); };\n    const int desiredBlocks = 2 * numSMs;\n\n    ForcePlan plan;\n    for (const int bpt : {4, 2, 1}) {\n        plan.bodiesPerThread = bpt;\n        plan.iBlocks = blocksFor(bpt);\n        if (plan.iBlocks >= desiredBlocks) {\n            return plan;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Not enough i blocks: distribute the j range over blockIdx.y as well.\n    const int totalTiles = (n + TILE - 1) / TILE;\n    const int wanted = (2 * desiredBlocks + plan.iBlocks - 1) / plan.iBlocks;\n    plan.chunkTiles = std::max(1, (totalTiles + std::max(1, wanted) - 1) / std::max(1, wanted));\n    plan.numChunks = (totalTiles + plan.chunkTiles - 1) / plan.chunkTiles;\n    return plan;\n}\n\nstatic void launchComputeForces(const ForcePlan& plan, const DeviceBodies& dev, double* partial, const int n) {\n    if (plan.numChunks > 1) {\n        const dim3 grid(plan.iBlocks, plan.numChunks);\n        computeForcesKernel<1, true><<<grid, TILE>>>(dev.px, dev.py, dev.pz, partial, nullptr, nullptr,                                                                                 plan.numChunks);\n        return;\n    }\n\n    switch (plan.bodiesPerThread) {\n    case 4:\n        computeForcesKernel<4, false><<<plan.iBlocks, TILE>>>(dev.px, dev.py, dev.pz, dev.vx, dev.vy, dev.vz, n, 0);\n        break;\n    case 2:\n        computeForcesKernel<2, false><<<plan.iBlocks, dev.vz, n, 0);\n        break;\n    }\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    //         // threads per block == shared-memory tile of bodies\nconstexpr int ENERGY_BLOCK = 256; // threads per block for the energy kernel\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    double x, y, z;\n    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                                               \\\n    do {                                                                                                               \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call); Body positions are\n// streamed through shared memory in tiles so that each position is read from global memory only once\n// per block; holding several bodies per thread additionally amortizes the shared-memory traffic and\n// exposes instruction-level parallelism, which matters for the long-latency FP64 pipeline.\n//\n// The inner loop visits j in ascending order for every i, i.e. forces are accumulated in exactly the\n// same order as in the sequential reference implementation.\n//\n// SPLIT == false: every block covers the whole j range and directly updates the velocities.\n// SPLIT == true:  the j range is additionally partitioned over blockIdx.y (needed to fill the GPU when\n//                 there are fewer bodies than desired threads); each block stores the partial force of\n//                 its j chunk, which reduceForcesKernel then sums up in ascending chunk order.\ntemplate <int BPT, bool SPLIT>\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(TILE) void computeForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ px,\n                                                            const double* __restrict__ py,\n                                                            const double* __restrict__ pz, double* __restrict__ vx,\n                                                            double* __restrict__ vy, double* __restrict__ vz,\n                                                            const int n, const int chunkTiles) {\n    __shared__ double sx[TILE];\n    __shared__ double sy[TILE];\n    __shared__ double sz[TILE];\n\n    const int tid = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    const int base = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * (TILE * BPT) + tid;\n\n    const int jBegin = SPLIT ? static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * chunkTiles * TILE : 0;\n    const int jEnd = SPLIT ? min(n, jBegin + chunkTiles * TILE) : n;\n\n    double xi[BPT], yi[BPT], zi[BPT];\n    double fx[BPT], fy[BPT], fz[BPT];\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int k = 0; k < BPT; ++k) {\n        const int i = base + k * TILE;\n        const bool active = i < n;\n        xi[k] = active ? px[i] : 0.0;\n        yi[k] = active ? py[i] : 0.0;\n        zi[k] = active ? pz[i] : 0.0;\n        fx[k] = 0.0;\n        fy[k] = 0.0;\n        fz[k] = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    for (int tileStart = jBegin; tileStart < jEnd; tileStart += TILE) {\n        const int count = min(TILE, jEnd - tileStart);\n        const int j = tileStart + tid;\n\n        __syncthreads();\n        if (tid < count) {\n            sx[tid] = px[j];\n            sy[tid] = py[j];\n            sz[tid] = pz[j];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n#pragma unroll 4\n        for (int jj = 0; jj < count; ++jj) {\n            const double xj = sx[jj];\n            const double yj = sy[jj];\n            const double zj = sz[jj];\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int k = 0; k < BPT; ++k) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[k];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[k];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[k];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                fx[k] += dx * invDist3;\n                fy[k] += dy * invDist3;\n                fz[k] += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int k = 0; k < BPT; ++k) {\n        const int i = base + k * TILE;\n        if (i < n) {\n            if (SPLIT) {\n                // vx/vy/vz point at the chunk-partial buffer: [chunk][component][body]\n                const size_t off = (static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * 3) * static_cast<size_t>(n) + i;\n                vx[off] = fx[k];\n                vx[off + n] = fy[k];\n                vx[off + 2 * n] = fz[k];\n            } else {\n                vx[i] += DT * fx[k];\n                vy[i] += DT * fy[k];\n                vz[i] += DT * fz[k];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Sums the per-chunk partial forces in ascending chunk (i.e. ascending j) order and advances the\n// velocities.\n__global__ void reduceForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ partial, double* __restrict__ vx,\n                                  double* __restrict__ vy, double* __restrict__ vz, const int n,\n                                  const int numChunks) {\n    const int i = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * static_cast<int>(blockDim.x) + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    if (i >= n) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;\n    for (int c = 0; c < numChunks; ++c) {\n        const size_t off = (static_cast<size_t>(c) * 3) * static_cast<size_t>(n) + i;\n        fx += partial[off];\n        fy += partial[off + n];\n        fz += partial[off + 2 * n];\n    }\n\n    vx[i] += DT * fx;\n    vy[i] += DT * fy;\n    vz[i] += DT * fz;\n}\n\n__global__ void integrateBodiesKernel(double* __restrict__ px, double* __restrict__ py, double* __restrict__ pz,\n                                      const double* __restrict__ vx, const double* __restrict__ vy,\n                                      const double* __restrict__ vz, const int n) {\n    const int i = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * static_cast<int>(blockDim.x) + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    if (i < n) {\n        px[i] += vx[i] * DT;\n        py[i] += vy[i] * DT;\n        pz[i] += vz[i] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\n// Potential energy contribution of body i against all j > i, again summed in ascending j order.\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(ENERGY_BLOCK) void potentialEnergyKernel(const double* __restrict__ px,\n                                                                      const double* __restrict__ py,\n                                                                      const double* __restrict__ pz,\n                                                                      double* __restrict__ partial, const int n) {\n    const int i = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * ENERGY_BLOCK + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    if (i >= n) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const double xi = px[i];\n    const double yi = py[i];\n    const double zi = pz[i];\n\n    double sum = 0.0;\n    for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n        const double dx = px[j] - xi;\n        const double dy = py[j] - yi;\n        const double dz = pz[j] - zi;\n        const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n        sum += 1.0 / dist;\n    }\n    partial[i] = sum;\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const DeviceBodies& dev) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    const int n = static_cast<int>(bodies.size());\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    if (n == 0) {\n        return energy;\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies), row sums computed on the GPU\n    double* dPartial = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dPartial, static_cast<size_t>(n) * sizeof(double)));\n    potentialEnergyKernel<<<(n + ENERGY_BLOCK - 1) / ENERGY_BLOCK, ENERGY_BLOCK>>>(dev.px, dev.py, dev.pz, dPartial, n);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n    std::vector<double> partial(n);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(partial.data(), dPartial, static_cast<size_t>(n) * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dPartial));\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        energy -= partial[i];\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Launch configuration for the force computation: use as many bodies per thread as the problem size\n// allows while still filling the GPU with blocks, and fall back to splitting the j loop across blocks\n// once there are not even enough bodies for one thread each.\nstruct ForcePlan {\n    int bodiesPerThread = 1;\n    int iBlocks = 0;\n    int numChunks = 1; // j chunks; > 1 selects the split kernel\n    int chunkTiles = 0;\n};\n\nstatic ForcePlan makeForcePlan(const int n, const int numSMs) {\n    const auto blocksFor = [&](const int bpt) { return (n + TILE * bpt - 1) / (TILE * bpt); };\n    const int desiredBlocks = 2 * numSMs;\n\n    ForcePlan plan;\n    for (const int bpt : {4, 2, 1}) {\n        plan.bodiesPerThread = bpt;\n        plan.iBlocks = blocksFor(bpt);\n        if (plan.iBlocks >= desiredBlocks) {\n            return plan;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Not enough i blocks: distribute the j range over blockIdx.y as well.\n    const int totalTiles = (n + TILE - 1) / TILE;\n    const int wanted = (2 * desiredBlocks + plan.iBlocks - 1) / plan.iBlocks;\n    plan.chunkTiles = std::max(1, (totalTiles + std::max(1, wanted) - 1) / std::max(1, wanted));\n    plan.numChunks = (totalTiles + plan.chunkTiles - 1) / plan.chunkTiles;\n    return plan;\n}\n\nstatic void launchComputeForces(const ForcePlan& plan, const DeviceBodies& dev, double* partial, const int n) {\n    if (plan.numChunks > 1) {\n        const dim3 grid(plan.iBlocks, plan.numChunks);\n        computeForcesKernel<1, true><<<grid, TILE>>>(dev.px, dev.py, dev.pz, partial, nullptr, nullptr,                                                                                 plan.numChunks);\n        return;\n    }\n\n    switch (plan.bodiesPerThread) {\n    case 4:\n         // threads per block == shared-memory tile of bodies\nconstexpr int ENERGY_BLOCK = 256; // threads per block for the energy kernel\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    double x, y, z;\n    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                                               \\\n    do {                                                                                                               \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                                          \\\n            exit(1);                                                                                                   \\\n        }                                                                                                              \\\n    } while (0)\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Structure-of-arrays body state living in device memory.\nstruct DeviceBodies {\n    double* px = nullptr;\n    double* py = nullptr;\n    double* pz = nullptr;\n    double* vx = nullptr;\n    double* vy = nullptr;\n    double* vz = nullptr;\n};\n\n// One thread computes the total force acting on one body. Body positions are streamed through shared\n// memory in tiles so that each position is read from global memory only once per block, which removes\n// almost all global-memory traffic from the O(n^2) inner loop.\n//\n// The inner loop visits j in ascending order, i.e. forces are accumulated in exactly the same order as\n// in the sequential reference implementation.\n//\n// SPLIT == false: every block covers the whole j range and directly updates the velocities.\n// SPLIT == true:  the j range is additionally partitioned over blockIdx.y (needed to fill the GPU when\n//                 there are fewer bodies than desired threads); each block stores the partial force of\n//                 its j chunk, which reduceForcesKernel then sums up in ascending chunk order.\ntemplate <bool SPLIT>\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(TILE) void computeForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ px,\n                                                            const double* __restrict__ py,\n                                                            const double* __restrict__ pz, double* __restrict__ vx,\n                                                            double* __restrict__ vy, double* __restrict__ vz,\n                                                            const int n, const int chunkTiles) {\n    __shared__ double sx[TILE];\n    __shared__ double sy[TILE];\n    __shared__ double sz[TILE];\n\n    const int tid = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    const int i = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * TILE + tid;\n\n    const int jBegin = SPLIT ? static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * chunkTiles * TILE : 0;\n    const int jEnd = SPLIT ? min(n, jBegin + chunkTiles * TILE) : n;\n\n    const bool active = i < n;\n    const double xi = active ? px[i] : 0.0;\n    const double yi = active ? py[i] : 0.0;\n    const double zi = active ? pz[i] : 0.0;\n\n    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;\n\n    for (int tileStart = jBegin; tileStart < jEnd; tileStart += TILE) {\n        const int count = min(TILE, jEnd - tileStart);\n\n        __syncthreads();\n        if (tid < count) {\n            sx[tid] = px[tileStart + tid];\n            sy[tid] = py[tileStart + tid];\n            sz[tid] = pz[tileStart + tid];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n#pragma unroll 4\n        for (int jj = 0; jj < count; ++jj) {\n            const double dx = sx[jj] - xi;\n            const double dy = sy[jj] - yi;\n            const double dz = sz[jj] - zi;\n            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n            fx += dx * invDist3;\n            fy += dy * invDist3;\n            fz += dz * invDist3;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (active) {\n        if (SPLIT) {\n            // vx points at the chunk-partial buffer, laid out as [chunk][component][body]\n            const size_t off = (static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * 3) * static_cast<size_t>(n) + i;\n            vx[off] = fx;\n            vx[off + n] = fy;\n            vx[off + 2 * n] = fz;\n        } else {\n            vx[i] += DT * fx;\n            vy[i] += DT * fy;\n            vz[i] += DT * fz;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Sums the per-chunk partial forces in ascending chunk (i.e. ascending j) order and advances the\n// velocities.\n__global__ void reduceForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ partial, double* __restrict__ vx,\n                                  double* __restrict__ vy, double* __restrict__ vz, const int n,\n                                  const int numChunks) {\n    const int i = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * static_cast<int>(blockDim.x) + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    if (i >= n) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;\n    for (int c = 0; c < numChunks; ++c) {\n        const size_t off = (static_cast<size_t>(c) * 3) * static_cast<size_t>(n) + i;\n        fx += partial[off];\n        fy += partial[off + n];\n        fz += partial[off + 2 * n];\n    }\n\n    vx[i] += DT * fx;\n    vy[i] += DT * fy;\n    vz[i] += DT * fz;\n}\n\n__global__ void integrateBodiesKernel(double* __restrict__ px, double* __restrict__ py, double* __restrict__ pz,\n                                      const double* __restrict__ vx, const double* __restrict__ vy,\n                                      const double* __restrict__ vz, const int n) {\n    const int i = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * static_cast<int>(blockDim.x) + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    if (i < n) {\n        px[i] += vx[i] * DT;\n        py[i] += vy[i] * DT;\n        pz[i] += vz[i] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\n// Potential energy contribution of body i against all j > i, again summed in ascending j order.\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(ENERGY_BLOCK) void potentialEnergyKernel(const double* __restrict__ px,\n                                                                      const double* __restrict__ py,\n                                                                      const double* __restrict__ pz,\n                                                                      double* __restrict__ partial, const int n) {\n    const int i = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * ENERGY_BLOCK + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    if (i >= n) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const double xi = px[i];\n    const double yi = py[i];\n    const double zi = pz[i];\n\n    double sum = 0.0;\n    for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n        const double dx = px[j] - xi;\n        const double dy = py[j] - yi;\n        const double dz = pz[j] - zi;\n        const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n        sum += 1.0 / dist;\n    }\n    partial[i] = sum;\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const DeviceBodies& dev) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    const int n = static_cast<int>(bodies.size());\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    if (n == 0) {\n        return energy;\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies), row sums computed on the GPU\n    double* dPartial = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dPartial, static_cast<size_t>(n) * sizeof(double)));\n    potentialEnergyKernel<<<(n + ENERGY_BLOCK - 1) / ENERGY_BLOCK, ENERGY_BLOCK>>>(dev.px, dev.py, dev.pz, dPartial, n);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n    std::vector<double> partial(n);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(partial.data(), dPartial, static_cast<size_t>(n) * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dPartial));\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        energy -= partial[i];\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Launch configuration for the force computation: one thread per body, plus - if that alone does not\n// produce enough blocks to fill the GPU - a partitioning of the j loop over a second grid dimension.\nstruct ForcePlan {\n    int iBlocks = 0;\n    int numChunks = 1; // number of j chunks; > 1 selects the split kernel\n    int chunkTiles = 0;\n};\n\nstatic ForcePlan makeForcePlan(const int n, const int numSMs) {\n    ForcePlan plan;\n    if (n <= 0) {\n        return plan;\n    }\n\n    const int totalTiles = (n + TILE - 1) / TILE;\n    plan.iBlocks = totalTiles;\n\n    // Aim for a few blocks per SM so that the FP64 pipelines stay saturated.\n    const int desiredBlocks = 4 * numSMs;\n    if (plan.iBlocks < desiredBlocks) {\n        const int wantedChunks = (desiredBlocks + plan.iBlocks - 1) / plan.iBlocks;\n        plan.chunkTiles = std::max(1, (totalTiles + wantedChunks - 1) / wantedChunks);\n        plan.numChunks = (totalTiles + plan.chunkTiles - 1) / plan.chunkTiles;\n    }\n    return plan;\n}\n\nstatic void launchComputeForces(const ForcePlan& plan, const DeviceBodies& dev, double* partial, const int n) {\n    if (plan.numChunks > 1) {\n        const dim3 grid(plan.iBlocks, plan.numChunks);\n        computeForcesKernel<true><<<grid, TILE>>>(dev.px, dev.py, dev.pz, partial, nullptr, nullptr, n,\n                                                  plan.chunkTiles);\n        reduceForcesKernel<<<(n + ENERGY_BLOCK - 1) / ENERGY_BLOCK, ENERGY_BLOCK>>>(partial, dev.vx, dev.vy, dev.vz, n,\n                                                                                    plan.numChunks);\n    } else {\n        computeForcesKernel<false><<<plan.iBlocks, TILE>>>(dev.px, dev.py, dev.pz, dev.vx, dev.vy, dev.vz, n, 0);\n    }\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"N-Body Simulation\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of bodies: %d\\n\", numBodies);\n    printf(\"Number of steps: %d\\n\", numSteps);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Initialize bodies\n    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);\n    randomizeBodies(bodies);\n\n    // Set up the GPU (device selection and context creation are one-off setup costs)\n    int device = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));\n    int numSMs = 1;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&numSMs, cudaDevAttrMultiProcessorCount, device));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr));\n\n    // Host-side structure-of-arrays staging buffers (pinned for fast transfers)\n    const size_t bytes = static_cast<size_t>(numBodies) * sizeof(double);\n    double* host = nullptr;\n    double* dPartial = nullptr;\n    DeviceBodies dev;\n    const ForcePlan plan = makeForcePlan(numBodies, numSMs);\n    if (numBodies > 0) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&host, 6 * bytes, cudaHostAllocDefault));\n        double* devMem = nullptr;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&devMem, 6 * bytes));\n        if (plan.numChunks > 1) {\n Body positions are streamed through shared\n// memory in tiles so that each position is read from global memory only once per block, which removes\n// almost all global-memory traffic from the O(n^2) inner loop.\n//\n// The inner loop visits j in ascending order, i.e. forces are accumulated in exactly the same order as\n// in the sequential reference implementation.\n//\n// SPLIT == false: every block covers the whole j range and directly updates the velocities.\n// SPLIT == true:  the j range is additionally partitioned over blockIdx.y (needed to fill the GPU when\n//                 there active ? py[i] : 0.0;\n    const double zi = active ? pz[i] : 0.0;\n\n    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;\n\n    for (int tileStart = jBegin; tileStart < jEnd; tileStart += TILE) {\n        const sz[tid] = pz[tileStart + tid];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n#pragma unroll 4\n        for (int jj = 0; jj < count; ++jj) {\n            const double dx = sx[jj] - xi;\n            const double dy = sy[jj] - yi;\n            const double dz = sz[jj] - zi;\n            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n            fx += dx * invDist3;\n            fy += dy * invDist3;\n            fz += dz * invDist3;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (active) {\n        if (SPLIT) {\n            // vx points at the chunk-partial buffer, laid out as [chunk][component][body]\n            const size_t off = DT * fx;\n            vy[i] += DT * fy;\n            vz[i] += DT * fz;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Sums the per-chunk partial forces in ascending chunk (i.e. ascending j) order and advances the\n//     return;\n    }\n\n    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;\n    for (int c = 0; c < numChunks; ++c) {\n        const size_t off = (static_cast<size_t>(c) * 3) * static_cast<size_t>(n) + i;\n         // threads per block == shared-memory tile of bodies\nconstexpr int ENERGY_BLOCK = 256; // threads per block for the energy kernel\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    double x, y, z;\n    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                                               \\\n    do {                                                                                                               \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call); Body positions are streamed through shared\n// memory in tiles so that each position is read from global memory only once per block, which removes\n// almost all global-memory traffic from the O(n^2) inner loop.\n//\n// The inner loop visits j in ascending order, i.e. forces are accumulated in exactly the same order as\n// in the sequential reference implementation.\n//\n// SPLIT == false: every block covers the whole j range and directly updates the velocities.\n// SPLIT == true:  the j range is additionally partitioned over blockIdx.y (needed to fill the GPU when\n//                 there are fewer bodies than desired threads); each block stores the partial force of\n//                 its j chunk, which reduceForcesKernel then sums up in ascending chunk order.\ntemplate <bool SPLIT>\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(TILE) void computeForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ px,\n                                                            const double* __restrict__ py,\n                                                            const double* __restrict__ pz, double* __restrict__ vx,\n                                                            double* __restrict__ vy, double* __restrict__ vz,\n                                                            const int n, const int chunkTiles) {\n    __shared__ double sx[TILE];\n    __shared__ double sy[TILE];\n    __shared__ double sz[TILE];\n\n    const int tid = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    const int i = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * TILE + tid;\n\n    const int jBegin = SPLIT ? static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * chunkTiles * TILE : 0;\n    const int jEnd = SPLIT ? min(n, jBegin + chunkTiles * TILE) : n;\n\n    const bool active = i < n;\n    const double xi = active ? px[i] : 0.0;\n    const double yi = active ? py[i] : 0.0;\n    const double zi = active ? pz[i] : 0.0;\n\n    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;\n\n    for (int tileStart = jBegin; tileStart < jEnd; tileStart += TILE) {\n        const int count = min(TILE, jEnd - tileStart);\n\n        __syncthreads();\n        if (tid < count) {\n            sx[tid] = px[tileStart + tid];\n            sy[tid] = py[tileStart + tid];\n            sz[tid] = pz[tileStart + tid];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n#pragma unroll 4\n        for (int jj = 0; jj < count; ++jj) {\n            const double dx = sx[jj] - xi;\n            const double dy = sy[jj] - yi;\n            const double dz = sz[jj] - zi;\n            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n            fx += dx * invDist3;\n            fy += dy * invDist3;\n            fz += dz * invDist3;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (active) {\n        if (SPLIT) {\n            // vx points at the chunk-partial buffer, laid out as [chunk][component][body]\n            const size_t off = (static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * 3) * static_cast<size_t>(n) + i;\n            vx[off] = fx;\n            vx[off + n] = fy;\n            vx[off + 2 * n] = fz;\n        } else {\n            vx[i] += DT * fx;\n            vy[i] += DT * fy;\n            vz[i] += DT * fz;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Sums the per-chunk partial forces in ascending chunk (i.e. ascending j) order and advances the\n// velocities.\n__global__ void reduceForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ partial, double* __restrict__ vx,\n                                  double* __restrict__ vy, double* __restrict__ vz, const int n,\n                                  const int numChunks) {\n    const int i = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * static_cast<int>(blockDim.x) + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    if (i >= n) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;\n    for (int c = 0; c < numChunks; ++c) {\n        const size_t off = (static_cast<size_t>(c) * 3) * static_cast<size_t>(n) + i;\n        fx += partial[off];\n        fy += partial[off + n];\n        fz += partial[off + 2 * n];\n    }\n\n    vx[i] += DT * fx;\n    vy[i] += DT * fy;\n    vz[i] += DT * fz;\n}\n\n__global__ void integrateBodiesKernel(double* __restrict__ px, double* __restrict__ py, double* __restrict__ pz,\n                                      const double* __restrict__ vx, const double* __restrict__ vy,\n                                      const double* __restrict__ vz, const int n) {\n    const int i = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * static_cast<int>(blockDim.x) + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    if (i < n) {\n        px[i] += vx[i] * DT;\n        py[i] += vy[i] * DT;\n        pz[i] += vz[i] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\n// Potential energy contribution of body row i (all j > i), summed in ascending j order. Row i has\n// n-1-i terms, so each thread handles the short row n-1-i together with the long row i to keep the\n// work per thread constant.\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(ENERGY_BLOCK) void potentialEnergyKernel(const double* __restrict__ px,\n                                                                      const double* __restrict__ py,\n                                                                      const double* __restrict__ pz,\n                                                                      double* __restrict__ partial, const int n) {\n    const int row = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * ENERGY_BLOCK + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    if (row >= (n + 1) / 2) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    for (int i = row, k = 0; k < 2; ++k, i = n - 1 - row) {\n        if (k == 1 && i == row) {\n            break; // odd n: the middle row is handled only once\n        }\n\n        const double xi = px[i];\n        const double yi = py[i];\n        const double zi = pz[i];\n\n        double sum = 0.0;\n        for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = px[j] - xi;\n            const double dy = py[j] - yi;\n            const double dz = pz[j] - zi;\n            const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            sum += 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n        partial[i] = sum;\n    }\n}\n\ndouble mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    if (n == 0) {\n        return energy;\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies), row sums computed on the GPU\n    double* dPartial = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dPartial, static_cast<size_t>(n) * sizeof(double)));\n    potentialEnergyKernel<<<(n + ENERGY_BLOCK - 1) / ENERGY_BLOCK, ENERGY_BLOCK>>>(dev.px, dev.py, dev.pz, dPartial, n);\n     printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps       Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Launch configuration for the force computation: one thread per body, plus - if that alone does not\n// produce enough blocks to fill the GPU - a partitioning of the j loop over a second grid dimension.\nstruct ForcePlan {\n    int iBlocks = 0;\n    int numChunks = 1; // number of j chunks; > 1 selects the split kernel\n    int chunkTiles = 0;\n};\n\nstatic ForcePlan makeForcePlan(const int n, const int numSMs) {\n    ForcePlan plan;\n    if (n <= 0) {\n        return plan;\n    }\n\n    const int totalTiles = (n + TILE - 1) / TILE;\n    plan.iBlocks = totalTiles;\n\n    // Aim for a few blocks per SM so that the FP64 pipelines stay saturated.\n    const int desiredBlocks = 4 * numSMs;\n    if (plan.iBlocks < desiredBlocks) {\n        const int wantedChunks = (desiredBlocks + plan.iBlocks - 1) / plan.iBlocks;\n        plan.chunkTiles = std::max(1, (totalTiles + wantedChunks - 1) / wantedChunks);\n        plan.numChunks = (totalTiles + plan.chunkTiles - 1) / plan.chunkTiles;\n    }\n    return plan;\n}\n\nstatic void launchComputeForces(const ForcePlan& plan, const DeviceBodies& dev, double* partial, const int n) {\n    if (plan.numChunks > 1) {\n        const dim3 grid(plan.iBlocks, plan.numChunks);\n        computeForcesKernel<true><<<grid, TILE>>>(dev.px, dev.py, dev.pz, partial, nullptr, nullptr, n,\n                                                  plan.chunkTiles);\n        reduceForcesKernel<<<(n + ENERGY_BLOCK - 1) / ENERGY_BLOCK, ENERGY_BLOCK>>>(partial, dev.vx, dev.vy, dev.vz, n,\n     double* devMem = nullptr;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&devMem, 6 * bytes));\n        if (plan.numChunks > 1) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dPartial, static_cast<size_t>(plan.numChunks)
